Fix 4 below covers a related case that was already broken. The build is clean and the engine's unit tests pass (0 of 244 failed), but I haven't run the game to look at these nodes, and none of the tests cover these drawing paths.\n\n**I couldn't watch the video.** `/app/.harbor_assets/` is empty, so `recording.mp4` isn't in the container. Instead I went through all the code that positions and rotates nodes when they're drawn. Where I could, I checked the rotation tables with a small script that compares them against the actual geometric rotations.\n\n### Fixes\n\n1. **Mesh nodes turned to the wrong angle** (`src/client/mesh.cpp`, `rotateMesh`). The rotation helper converted the angle from radians to degrees when it should have gone degrees to radians. Every mesh rotated at draw time came out at a meaningless angle. That covers degree-rotated meshes always, and facedir or wallmounted meshes whenever the mesh cache is off, which is the default.\n\n2. **Scaled box-shaped nodes drawn far from where they were placed** (`src/client/content_mapblock.cpp`, `drawAutoLightedCuboid`). The node's world position was added before the box was multiplied by `visual_scale`, so the position got scaled too. Any nodebox or allfaces node (e.g. leaves) with `visual_scale ≠ 1` ended up at `position × scale`, and the error grows the further it is from the world origin. The position is now added after scaling.\n\n3. **Connected nodeboxes (fences, walls, panes) with front/back swapped** (`nodebox_connection_dirs`). The API, collision and selection code all treat `front` as Z− and `back` as Z+, but the renderer had them the other way round. These nodes therefore drew their front/back arms toward the wrong neighbour, which also made what you see disagree with what you collide with.\n\n4. **Wallmounted meshes with the mesh cache and smooth lighting both on** (`drawMeshNode`). The renderer took the base mesh, which the cache had already rotated, and rotated it again by the raw wallmounted value instead of a facedir. It now copies the matching cached mesh, which is already rotated correctly.\n\n### Checked and found correct\n- Placement and turning: the facedir and wallmounted tables (`rotateAlongYAxis`, the Lua `dir_to_facedir` / `dir_to_wallmounted` functions).\n- How fixed and wallmounted nodeboxes are rotated, and how textures are mapped on all 24 facedir orientations (checked by simulation).\n- The torch, sign, plant (including rooted plants), fire and rail drawing code.\n\nBecause the video was missing, I can't confirm these are exactly the defects it shows.
